15.FC Orenbúrg              23   4  7 12  24-36  19
16.FC Sóči                  23   2  3 18  20-53   9  [P]

FC Hímki were denied the RPL license and relegated to First League.
Torpédo (Moskvá) were excluded from RPL for attempted match-fixing.
Akrón play home games at Krýĺja Sovétov's home stadium in Samara (100 km south-east of Togliatti).
Dinámo (Mahačkalá) play home games in Kaspíjsk (the satellite city south-east of Mahačkalá).
Due to renovation of their home stadium, Pari NN played home games at various locations until Round 12.

  renovation of their home stadium, Torpédo play home games in Hímki (the satellite

Sókol (Sarátov) weren't relegated to fill the spot vacated by the liquidation of FC Hímki.
